## `AFG-5819-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** OVI-Spectrocolorimeter Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Print Rub Abrasion Resistance & Crease Burst-Resistant
- **Intro:** OVI-Spectrocolorimeter Metrologist — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Print Rub Abrasion Resistance & Crease Burst-Resistant — Pre-Start Verification and PPE Inspection: Voltage: 220V AC ±10% (198-242V); Grounding resistance ≤ 0.5 Ω; Ambient temperature 23°C ± 2°C; Relative humidity 50% ± 5%.
- **Key tolerance:** Voltage: 220V AC ±10% (198-242V); Grounding resistance ≤ 0.5 Ω; Ambient temperature 23°C ± 2°C; Relative humidity 50% ± 5%.
- **Standards:** `ISO 11664-4:2019`, `ASTM D5264-98(2020) Standard Practice for Abrasion Resistance of`, `TAPPI T403 om-15 Bursting Strength of Paper`, `ISO 9001:2015`
- **Detail pages:** [EN](https://sop.go2abroad.space/en/sop/AFG-5819-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [中文](https://sop.go2abroad.space/zh/sop/AFG-5819-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [DE](https://sop.go2abroad.space/de/sop/AFG-5819-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [日本語](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ja/sop/AFG-5819-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [FR](https://sop.go2abroad.space/fr/sop/AFG-5819-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [ES](https://sop.go2abroad.space/es/sop/AFG-5819-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [PT](https://sop.go2abroad.space/pt/sop/AFG-5819-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [IT](https://sop.go2abroad.space/it/sop/AFG-5819-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [한국어](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ko/sop/AFG-5819-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [العربية](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ar/sop/AFG-5819-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [हिन्दी](https://sop.go2abroad.space/hi/sop/AFG-5819-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html)
